- **scrumia-effect**: The typed-effect discipline — describe before execute, failure as a value, the effect boundary, and which approach to reach for (Result, Either, IO/suspend, or effect.website). Plugs in app by app. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-effect))
- **scrumia-functional-programming**: The Functional Programming paradigm, language-neutral — purity, total functions, referential transparency, immutability by default, composition over inheritance, and effect discipline. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-functional-programming))

- **scrumia-gradle**: Gradle the build tool — Kotlin DSL over Groovy, version catalogs, the shape of convention plugins, lazy task configuration, the build and configuration caches, composite builds, pluginManagement. - **scrumia-html-css**: The HTML, CSS and accessibility capability — semantic elements over ARIA roles, the element follows the purpose, and tests query what the user meets. - **scrumia-impl-reactjs**: The implementation slot, for React 19 apps — Server Components by default, Actions and useActionState for mutations and forms, derived state calculated during render. - **scrumia-impl-rust**: The implementation slot, for Rust apps — invalid states made unrepresentable, typed errors per layer, one test per invariant. Plugs in app by app. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-impl-rust))
- **scrumia-impl-solidjs**: The implementation slot, for SolidJS apps — fine-grained reactivity without React reflexes, behaviour-first component tests. Plugs in app by app. ([tibs245/scrumia](https://github.com/tibs245/scrumia/tree/HEAD/plugins/scrumia-impl-solidjs))
- **scrumia-kotlin**: Idiomatic Kotlin language rules — val/var, null-safety and platform types, coroutines and Flow, data/sealed/value classes, object vs companion vs top-level, and visibility. - **scrumia-kotlin-multiplatform-mobile**: The Kotlin Multiplatform Mobile composition preset — KMP-specific rules (expect/actual across source sets, source-set layout, iOS/Android split, Cocoapods and Swift interop, target declaration, KMP-shaped Gradle wiring). Re-exports six satellites (scrumia-kotlin, scrumia-gradle, scrumia-ktor, scrumia-material3, scrumia-effect, scrumia-functional-programming) by configuration. - **scrumia-ktor**: HTTP server and client rules for a Ktor codebase — routing declared as a tree, content negotiation installed once, a long-lived HttpClient, named authentication providers, tests on the in-memory engine, configuration outside the code, and a stated choice between WebSockets and SSE. Ships a run reviewer that turns a finished run into the evals it should have had, and an advisory onboarding skill that reports which optional tools this session can invoke and what a run does without each. - **sdlc-graph-engineering-install**: Installs graph engineering into a project that has none: turns an existing process — skills, a runbook, a CI config, or a description — into a guarded graph with typed nodes, total exit guards, bounded retry loops, a run-state file, a skipped-step ledger, and its own auditor. Domain-agnostic; needs no other plugin installed. ([RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering](https://github.com/RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-graph-engineering-install))
- **sdlc-graph-viewer**: The run viewer for sdlc-graph — one zero-dependency page that renders parallel milestone agents, live or as a self-contained snapshot. Reads the run-directory layout: docs/graph-runs/&lt;run-id&gt;/{state.json,journals/}. ([RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering](https://github.com/RonMizrahi/sdlc-graph-engineering/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-graph-viewer))
- **sdlc-harness**: Full SDLC orchestration with 19 agents (harness, analyst, scaffolder, implementer, deployer, documenter, QA coordinator, 8 QA reviewers, RAI reviewer, release manager) and 16 skills covering code quality, security, architecture, testing, scaffolding, and Azure cloud patterns. ([source](.))
- **sdlc-harness**: Agentic SDLC harness — five-agent shape (planner/builder/reviewer/security/release), feature lifecycle slash commands, deterministic scripts, optional visual QA, optional Codex-CLI cross-model review. ([manish-dharod/sdlc-harness](https://github.com/manish-dharod/sdlc-harness))
- **sdlc-kit**: Spec-Driven Development for Claude Code. 11 skills for the daily PR loop — init, PRDs, ADRs, SDD specs (EARS), tasks, git worktrees, code reviews (Code Reviewer + AppSec dual-axis), retrospectives. Includes a PostToolUse hook that auto-indexes every .md save into a SQLite tracker. Self-contained HTML dashboard (Kanban + branch graph) included. ([anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community/tree/HEAD/plugins/core))
- **sdlc-kit-extended**: 11 optional skills layered on top of sdlc-kit core. Adds governance (TRDs, epics, milestones, steering), architecture & domain modeling (C4 diagrams in 3 levels, API contracts in 4 styles, DDD aggregates/events/contracts, design system tokens), and post-delivery analysis (incidents with auto-timestamps, traceability matrix, BFS impact analysis). Requires sdlc-kit core. ([anthropics/claude-plugins-community](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-community/tree/HEAD/plugins/extended))
- **sdlc-knowledge-base**: Filesystem-based project knowledge base — librarian agent, hash-tracked indexes, cross-library queries with priming + synthesis, audit log, parallel map-reduce bulk ingest, ingest/query/lint operations ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-knowledge-base))
- **sdlc-lang-javascript**: JavaScript/TypeScript-specific validation and patterns ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-lang-javascript))
- **sdlc-lang-kotlin**: Kotlin language expert — idiomatic Kotlin 2.x, coroutines & structured concurrency, Flow, sealed/data/value classes, generics, KSP, KMP basics (pairs with sdlc-team-android) ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-lang-kotlin))
- **sdlc-lang-python**: Python-specific validation, patterns, and expert agents ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-lang-python))
- **sdlc-lang-swift**: Swift language expert — idiomatic Swift 6.2, strict concurrency, value semantics, generics, macros, SwiftPM (pairs with sdlc-team-ios) ([SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices](https://github.com/SteveGJones/ai-first-sdlc-practices/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-lang-swift))
- **sdlc-loop**: The six-stage AI-native SDLC playbook — intent, design with Gherkin scenarios, plan and build, test, deploy, maintain — as slash commands with opt-in hooks and a reflect step. ([meteoFurletov/skills](https://github.com/meteoFurletov/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-loop))
